| T5410 | [Poster selection rework: athletic open-play frame + editable preview](tasks/T5410-poster-selection-rework.md) | 8 | 7 | 1.1 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-03 prod). Follows the DONE clearest-frame-posters epic. User-ranking study on real prod reels proved the shipped byte-size "clearest frame" heuristic is WORSE than random (Spearman -0.54) and samples the WRONG zone: the auto-spotlight moment (~0-2s, contested/occluded) ranks WORST as a cover (mean rank 0.80, never #1 across 5 reels), open-play slow-mo ranks BEST (0.38, #1 in 4/5). Zone-weighted score (10*open_play + 2*size + 1.5*ball + 1*(1-occ)), NOT a learned vector (overfits ~25 labels). 3 parts: (1) new selection algo replacing extract_clearest_frame_jpeg; (2) move detection compute to the overlay phase / Modal-free CPU pass so publish adds NO extra Modal (extend sampling past the 2s spotlight to the open-play window); (3) UX: completed draft exposes "View/edit preview image" (scrub-to-frame + upload-custom, surgical write). L-tier: Architect gate + Migration (poster_frame_time) + frontend override modal + backfill. Renumbered from T5380 (collided with the merged CropOverlay task). See T5410-design.md (Stage 2, awaiting approval). |

| T4150 | [PWA In-Session Update](tasks/T4150-pwa-in-session-update.md) | 5 | 2 | 2.5 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-03 prod). Installed PWA never applies a new build in-session: sw.js auto-updates in the background but the app never imports `virtual:pwa-register`, so a long-lived PWA runs a stale bundle until its next full load. (Originally blamed for the "unframed 16:9 clips in ranking" prod report; T4160 later proved that was a sweep auto-export bug - the update gap is real regardless.) Fix: switch VitePWA to `registerType: 'prompt'`, call `registerSW({ onNeedRefresh })` from the app entry to show a persistent "New version available - Refresh" toast wired to `updateSW(true)` (reuses shared Toast, deduped), plus a visibility-triggered `registration.update()` re-check (rate-limited; re-surfaces a dismissed prompt) for long-lived sessions - chosen over setInterval, which browsers freeze in backgrounded PWAs. Frontend-only. |

| T4000 | [Parallelize Game Video Fetch With /load](tasks/T4000-parallelize-game-video-fetch.md) | 6 | 4 | 1.5 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-06-26, frontend): opening a game was two sequential round-trips (`/api/games/{id}/load` returned the presigned `playback_url`, then the `<video>` started fetching ~762ms total). | T6360 | [Pack poster + rich metadata into every downloaded video](tasks/T6360-download-metadata-cover-art.md) | 5 | 4 | 1.3 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-16 prod). Platform sweep (iOS share-sheet -> Instagram/social) verified before this deploy per the task's own stated top risk. Filed 2026-08-02. Downloads leave the app as bare MP4s: no cover art, no title/author/album, container date = the download moment (so a December game files under today in any date-sorted timeline), and no machine-readable attribution to pair with the burned-in outro. We already generate a good poster at publish (final_videos/posters/{filename}.jpg, T5280/T4890) and never write it into the file the user takes home. Fix = ONE serve-time stamping pass with `-c copy` (no re-encode, no quality change) right after the T3950 outro append, so stored R2 objects stay byte-identical, playback/streaming/ranking are untouched, and every EXISTING reel gets it with no backfill. Writes: attached_pic cover art + title/artist/album_artist/album/date+creation_time (the GAME date, not export date)/comment/copyright/genre, keeping `+faststart`. Deliberately NOT written: location/GPS (children at recurring venues; the coordinate travels with every re-share), chapters, any pixel or audio change. Covers all 4 egress points; path 3 is a real pre-existing gap - the share-page download (SharedVideoOverlay) fetches the presigned R2 URL directly and today gets NEITHER metadata NOR the branded outro, so it gets routed through a backend endpoint. T4945 collection downloads must reuse the same helper. Known-and-accepted limits to document rather than fight: Windows Explorer ignores `covr` and makes its own thumbnail; social re-uploads strip most tags - the win is the user's own filesystem/camera roll/AirDrop/Plex/Apple ecosystem. Missing poster or missing game date -> omit that field, never fabricate. |
| T7000 | [`_js_path()` loud-failure guard no longer raises at Fly deploy depth](tasks/T7000-js-path-guard-broken-fly-depth.md) | 4 | 2 | 2.0 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-16 prod). Merged 2026-08-15 (`e7e957b5`). Root cause: the test's mock used a Windows-style path (`C:\app\...`), which resolves differently than a real POSIX Fly path under Linux CI's `.resolve()` — the depth check never fired, so the guard never raised. The guard itself was correct all along. Fixed the mock to be POSIX-absolute; `known-failures.md` row removed (5 independent confirmations resolved: master, T6990, T7040, T4946, T7020). |

| T7040 | [Collection download fails with "TypeError: Failed to fetch"](tasks/T7040-collection-download-failed-to-fetch.md) | 8 | 5 | 1.6 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-16 prod). | T7370 | [Overlay stuck loading a working video rendered by the CPU mock encoder](tasks/T7370-overlay-mock-encoder-faststart.md) | 6 | 2 | 3.0 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-24 prod). Found live-testing T4330 in a Modal-disabled dev container: Overlay could never load a Framing export's output (`moov atom at EOF and tail-range parse failed`), and the "Reset to spotlight" pill rendered over the stuck spinner before the video ever loaded. Root cause: `local_processors.py`'s two CPU/no-GPU fallback encoders (used by every /dotask container and any local dev session without a GPU) were the only encoders in the codebase missing `-movflags +faststart` — added it to both. Separately gated `isPastSpotlight` on `duration > 0` so the Reset pill can't show before Overlay's own video has loaded. Fixed directly on master (small, fully tested, interactive session) rather than a feature branch. |
| T7380 | [Global presigned-URL cache outlives its own signature expiry](tasks/T7380-presigned-url-cache-outlives-expiry.md) | 5 | 2 | 2.5 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-24 prod). Found live-testing T4330: a freshly-uploaded game's poster never generates, backend log shows `HTTP error 403 Forbidden` on the presigned R2 URL ffmpeg uses to grab a frame. Root cause: `generate_presigned_url_global`'s process-wide `TTLCache` has a fixed 3.5h eviction TTL independent of each call's own `expires_in` — two `poster.py` callers pass `expires_in=3600` (1h), so once cached the URL is replayed for up to 2.5 extra hours after R2 stops honoring its signature, guaranteeing 403s in that window. Confirmed via the stale `X-Amz-Date` reused 90 minutes past its own expiry in the backend log. Fix: store per-entry expiry and validate on read instead of trusting the outer TTL to always exceed every caller's `expires_in`. |
| T7390 | [CropOverlay drag/resize preventDefault silently no-ops on touch](tasks/T7390-cropoverlay-passive-touch-preventdefault.md) | 4 | 3 | 1.3 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-24 prod). Found via a user-supplied browser log after a live Framing export: `Unable to preventDefault inside passive event listener invocation` from `CropOverlay.jsx`. Root cause: `handleCropPointerDown`/`handleResizePointerDown` are bound via `onTouchStart`, which React has attached as a passive listener since React 17 — `preventDefault()` inside is a silent no-op, so touch-drag on the crop box/resize handles does not actually block the browser's default scroll/bounce behavior. The same file already has the fix pattern (`handleStraightenPointerDown`'s Pointer Events + `touch-action:none`, precedent T5640/T5644/T5450) — the crop-drag code predates that migration. Fix: convert to Pointer Events + `setPointerCapture`, matching the established pattern in the same file. |
| T7290 | [Games tab organizes by match date, not upload date](tasks/T7290-games-tab-match-date-organization.md) | 7 | 3 | 2.3 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-24 prod). Direct user report 2026-08-19: month headers say "June 2026 (5 games)" over tiles titled "at Sporting Mar 21" — the tab groups/sorts/labels by `created_at` (upload), which the user does not think in. `game_date` already exists end to end (games column, REQUIRED at creation in GameDetailsModal, editable in EditGameModal, in the list payload, already the date in the display name) — no capture, migration, or backfill needed, it just is not used for organization. Three call sites: `groupGamesByMonth` (ProjectManager.jsx ~L109-128), `ORDER BY g.created_at DESC` (games.py ~L941), tile footer date (GameTile.jsx ~L260). Fix = group/sort by match date on both ends; **drop the tile footer date entirely** (user decision — date is already in the title and now the header); NULL `game_date` (legacy/materialized rows) falls back to `created_at` for placement only, keeping the existing loud backend warning. Watch the timezone trap: `game_date` is date-only TEXT, parse as local calendar date so a 1st-of-month game cannot slide into the prior month. Out of scope: schema change, Reel Drafts grouping (already match-date), "Continue where you left off" (genuinely activity recency), sort-toggle UI (would be persisted view state). |

| T7320 | [Games-tab cache warming dead since T5681](tasks/T7320-games-cache-warming-observer-dead.md) | 6 | 1 | 6.0 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-24 prod). | T7340 | [Restore admin TSV annotation import/export in production](tasks/T7340-restore-admin-tsv-annotation-import.md) | 4 | 2 | 2.0 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-24 prod). | T7350 | [Mobile share skips native OS sheet (UA-sniff regression)](tasks/T7350-mobile-share-ua-sniff-regression.md) | 6 | 3 | 2.0 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-24 prod). Root cause traced via git history, not yet device-reproduced: `DownloadsPanel.webShareReel` gates the native-share attempt on `useWebShare()`'s `isMobile`, a raw `navigator.userAgent` regex (`isMobileDevice()`) — the SAME UA-sniff landmine T6300 (2026-08-01) already found misdetecting a touchscreen device as desktop and deliberately removed from this exact codepath. T5220's later fix (`8a051985`, 2026-08-08) reintroduced it to solve a real but different bug (desktop Chromium exposing `navigator.share()` for URL-only shares). Fix: swap the regex for the matchMedia-based `useIsCoarsePointer()`/`useIsMobile()` (`hooks/useIsMobile.js`) already standardized on elsewhere in the codebase — fixes mobile without reopening the desktop regression. |

## Single-Server Priority (2026-07-18; durability re-escalated 2026-07-24) — Bugs

| T6090 | [`scripts/delete_user.py` orphans the T5840 credit ledger](tasks/T6090-delete-user-script-orphans-credit-ledger.md) | 4 | 1 | 4.0 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-30 prod). Found 2026-07-27 while wiping staging to copy prod accounts down. delete_user.py purges the user's R2 prefix and identity rows but its delete list (delete_one, ~145-171) never touches T5840's `credits`/`credit_transactions`/`credit_reservations`, so deleting a user orphans the ledger permanently AND re-creating a user with the same user_id silently inherits the stale balance. OBSERVED: after deleting all 7 staging accounts and copying imankh/arshia down from prod, they came up with 395 and 28 credits - leftover STAGING balances attached to freshly-copied PROD accounts. LATENT on prod only because prod is still at postgres v018 and the tables do not exist there; it becomes capable of orphaning real financial records the moment the pending deploy runs v019, which is why this is worth fixing with that deploy. Blast radius is limited and verified (do NOT re-derive): the APP paths are correct - auth.py `_purge_user_data` explicitly purges all three (T5840 BLOCKING-4), and both DELETE /api/privacy/delete-account (CCPA) and DELETE /api/auth/user delegate to it with comments saying not to duplicate; sibling operator scripts reset_all_accounts.py:279, reset-test-user.py:339 and delete_all_guests.py:124 all handle credits too. delete_user.py is the single tool missed when T5840 landed - so NO user-facing or compliance defect. Fix: add the three tables matching the sibling scripts, honour the --dry-run "would delete N rows" contract, follow reset-test-user.py's ordering for FKs, and guard for a pre-v019 destination via to_regclass (pattern already used in copy_user_between_envs.py, commit 2eecb37e). Second deliverable: sweep the orphaned credits rows already on staging (7 deleted users incl. two synthetic e2e_* ids) and report the count; prod needs no sweep and must not be touched. |
| T6040 | [A reader on a conflicted machine now browses stale data silently - give the reader a quiet "newer version available / Reload"](tasks/T6040-reader-sees-stale-data-silently.md) | 5 | 2 | 2.5 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-28 prod). Follow-up to T5960+T6010 (both merged 2026-07-27). Gating the alarm on write-attempt correctly stopped telling a passive reader "Could not save to the cloud" for work they never did - but that banner was the ONLY user-visible signal that the machine is serving STALE data AND the only affordance to fix it. Traced 2026-07-27, do NOT re-derive: (1) the read path never refreshes - ensure_database() (database.py:653-663) restores only when local_version is None, i.e. restore-if-ABSENT not restore-if-newer, because a per-request HEAD costs 20s+ cold; a conflicted machine HAS a local version (merely behind R2) so the branch never fires; (2) the staleness is DURABLE - get_local_db_version falls back to reading the version out of the DB file and set_local_db_version persists it there deliberately, so cycling the process does not heal it; (3) Retry genuinely works and must not be "fixed" - POST /api/retry-sync -> _retry_resolve_conflict (health.py:225) calls confirm_current_before_write on both user.sqlite and the profile DB (real restore-if-newer), clears markers, reports honestly (NB middleware/db_sync.py: retry_pending_sync is a DIFFERENT background path that only re-refuses); (4) syncStore.retrySyncToR2 already branches on data.restored -> stashRestoredNotice + reloadPage, so no backend change is needed. KEY ASYMMETRY to encode: `conflict` means r2_version > local, R2 is AHEAD, the reader sees STALE data and needs to know; `failed` means the upload did not land, local is AHEAD holding unsynced changes, the reader sees the NEWEST data and needs nothing - so this task changes the reader treatment of `conflict` ONLY and must not touch T6010's `failed` gating. Fix: two presentations of one state split by the existing hasAttemptedWrite signal - writer keeps today's red alarm + Retry unchanged; reader gets a quiet non-alarm "A newer version of your work is available" + Reload. Do not show a reader the "your local changes were replaced" notice - they have no local changes to lose, which is the same bug class T5960 fixed. Frontend-only. |
| T6010 | [The `failed` sync alarm has T5960's staleness bug too - gate it on write-attempt](tasks/T6010-gate-failed-sync-alarm-on-write-attempt.md) | 4 | 1 | 4.0 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-28 prod). Follow-up to T5960 (merged 2026-07-27, e90633d7), which gated the `conflict` alarm on write-attempt but deliberately left `failed` out of scope. Verified in the backend, do NOT re-derive: sync_status_header (db_sync.py:313) returns "failed" whenever has_sync_failed(user_id) - a property of the USER, evaluated for ANY session; `.sync_failed` (database.py:123-144) is set after the bounded re-drain definitively gives up and cleared ONLY by a later successful sync (database.py:1463), on the exact same path that clears `.sync_conflict` one line above (database.py:1462). The two markers are EQUALLY sticky - `failed` does not self-heal faster. So a user whose write failed in session A, closing the tab and returning, gets "Could not save to the cloud / Some changes didn't reach the cloud" on a passive load in session B - the shape T5960 removed for `conflict`. The one counter-argument (a `failed` marker can be set out-of-band by the export worker on the user's behalf, so it is arguably worth showing to a non-writer) does NOT hold: if the export started in THIS session, POST /api/exports/framing already armed the gate; if it started in a previous session, that IS the bug. Extend T5960's existing gate in SyncStatusIndicator.jsx to cover `failed`; backend untouched; `pending`/`offline` unchanged (evaluate `pending` and recommend, but do not action). Frontend-only, ~1 file + tests. |
| T6020 | [Write-attempt gate's URL denylist cannot classify /api/projects/{id}/state - mark lifecycle writes at the call site](tasks/T6020-lifecycle-writes-marked-at-call-site.md) | 3 | 2 | 1.5 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-28 prod). Follow-up to T5960 (merged 2026-07-27, e90633d7). T5960 classifies "did this session attempt a genuine user write?" by matching the request URL against denylists in syncStore.js (NON_GESTURE_API_PREFIXES/_EXACT/_PATTERNS). Structural flaw: PATCH /api/projects/{id}/state is fired BOTH on project load (useProjectLoader.js:123, bookkeeping, no intent) AND on a real mode-switch gesture (App.jsx:589,650) - the SAME pathname, so a URL matcher cannot separate them and project-open still arms the gate, letting a stale conflict surface on what feels like a passive load. The only URL-level discriminator is the `update_last_opened` query param, considered and REJECTED in T5960 review (couples the frontend gate to a backend param name that can change and silently break the exclusion). Second problem: the coupling is invisible at the call site - a new lifecycle write must register itself in a list in another file and nothing fails if it is forgotten; that already bit T5960 once (useExportRecovery.js's two mount-time writes were missed and needed a follow-up commit). Fix: apiFetch.js is `fetch(url, {credentials:'include', ...options})` so options pass straight through and fetch ignores unknown RequestInit keys - mark the ~8 lifecycle call sites with one explicit greppable key and DELETE the URL denylists. Keep the denylist failure direction (a forgotten mark = stale alarm = status quo; a forgotten allowlist entry = a real writer silently loses their alarm), so do NOT invert to an allowlist. Matches CLAUDE.md refactoring rule 6 (greppability over registry indirection). Frontend-only. |
| T5970 | [Hot-path SELECTs of not-yet-migrated columns 500 every user in the deploy->migrate window](tasks/T5970-migration-window-column-guard-audit.md) | 8 | 3 | 4.0 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-28 prod). Found by the 2026-07-26 unit+staging sweep. Versioned migrations do NOT auto-run on deploy (CLAUDE.md), so between deploy and the admin migrate every per-user SQLite DB is at the OLD schema while NEW code is live. A hot-path SELECT naming a column the pending migration adds then raises `sqlite3.OperationalError: no such column` for EVERY user - a 500, not a degraded read. ONE instance found and already fixed (commit 6cc0c2a1: get_overlay_data named working_videos.detections_data from profile_db/v027); the CLASS was never audited. Sanctioned guard already exists and is documented for exactly this: column_exists() (database.py:164) + T5630s _has_stage_columns (exports.py:44), with correct call sites in clips.py/framing.py/multi_clip.py. Audit every profile_db + user_db ADD COLUMN migration (v005 highlight_shape, v007, v008 game_ids, v009/v010 ranking, v013 auto_export_attempts, v015 last_playhead_position, v016 clip_game_start_time, v024 poster_filename, v025 slowmo_section_*, v026 shared_by, user_db/v004) for unguarded reachable-before-migrate reads. Postgres is exempt (ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS + shared DB). Do NOT blanket-guard - justify each guard per column. SECOND deliverable: the 500 reproduced on a REAL local profile DB, so establish whether v027..head has actually been run on staging AND prod and run it if not (memory: only versions > PRAGMA user_version apply; v017 once crashed for 4 prod users on the tuple row-factory). Add a read-only migration-status endpoint if cheap - that closes the known tracking gap. |
| T6030 | [Close the migration-window class - v025 residual + a structural guard](tasks/T6030-close-migration-window-class-v025-and-guard.md) | 7 | 3 | 2.3 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-28 prod). Follow-up to T5970 (merged 2026-07-27). T5970 fixed two migration-window 500s (v027 detections_data on the overlay read, v026 shared_by on the bootstrap quests read) and audited every profile_db/user_db ADD COLUMN, but left ONE documented residual and did not make the class structurally un-reopenable. Residual = v025 final_videos.slowmo_section_start/end, named explicitly at TWO sites: downloads.py:1438 SELECT (POST /publish/{project_id}) and export/overlay.py:186 INSERT (render/finalize). Both 500 on a below-v025 DB; T5970 left them because they are deliberate export/publish GESTURES not a mass-on-load path (sound reasoning - hence Impact 7 not 9), but the INSERT site means NO export completes at all until the migrate runs. Fix both with column_exists (database.py:164); for the INSERT omit the columns from BOTH the column list and the positional VALUES tuple - do not insert NULL into a column that does not exist. Second half is the durable one: T5970's audit was a ONE-TIME manual sweep, so the next ADD COLUMN silently reopens the hole. Add a regression test that builds a below-head profile DB WITH ROWS (schema-only is not acceptable - reference_migration_runner_rowfactory records a migration that passed schema tests and still crashed on real rows for 4 prod users) and drives the hot-path reads asserting no OperationalError; prefer deriving the below-head schema from the migration registry so future versions extend coverage free, but an explicit version with a stated tradeoff is acceptable. NOT in scope: running migrations on staging/prod (no creds, never target prod - supervisor owns it). Use the query_counter fixture to prove no PRAGMA-per-row regression. Backend baseline on merged master 2026-07-27: 2396 passed, 16 skipped, 0 failed. |
| T6080 | [A corner-resize drag can exit circle-edit in mobile fullscreen](tasks/T6080-corner-handle-click-exits-circle-edit.md) | 5 | 1 | 5.0 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-28 prod). Found by T6000 (merged 2026-07-27) while adding real-browser corner-drag coverage. HighlightOverlay.jsx swallows the synthetic trailing click on the ellipse BODY (stopClick at :204, wired at :339 as onClick when tapToToggle) so a tap never also reaches video tap-nav - but the four corner handles (:583-589) bind only onPointerDown. OverlayModeView.jsx:628 binds onClick={mobileFs ? handleVideoAreaTap : undefined}, so in mobile fullscreen a click bubbling out of a corner handle reaches handleVideoAreaTap and dismisses circle-edit right after the user finishes resizing. Blast radius is NARROW (hence Impact 5 not 8): real touch drags do not synthesize a click, so typical phones are unaffected - it bites desktop-in-mobileFs, device emulation, and input stacks that synthesize compatibility mouse events; confirm the exact set rather than trusting that sentence. Fix is one attribute but sits in a hot pointer path, so reproduce in a REAL browser first (jsdom is insufficient - T5390 passed jsdom and failed on real touch), then check every interactive overlay child either swallows its trailing click or has a stated reason not to (the move lever at :535 already does). Re-run the whole T5610 suite; pre-existing playback flakes there (test 3 both contexts, T5450 loop) are NOT yours. |

| T6110 | [Three staging specs act on a placeholder before the video is ready](tasks/T6110-specs-act-before-video-ready.md) | 6 | 3 | 2.0 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-28 prod). Pairs with T6100 but lands REGARDLESS of what it concludes - a spec asserting on a not-yet-hydrated stage is wrong even if the app is fast. T4550 (:59) does `await expect(cropBox).toBeVisible()` then immediately drags, but the crop box is visible WHILE the video still loads, so the drag is measured against a stage with no video geometry; T5676 (:144) waits 240s for the overlay-video-stage aspect-ratio signal; bug38-autoselect (:63) times out on locator.click at 8s. Fix = gate on the real ready-signal per surface, never on element visibility or a fixed sleep; make a non-hydrated stage fail with a message SAYING the video never hydrated rather than a misleading domain assertion; rewrite T4550's T5380 comment to distinguish "never became ready" from "ready but first drag dropped" (keep the guard, make it honest). Do NOT fix by raising timeouts. Reuse helpers/appReady.js waitForAppReady + the FIXTURE-CONTRACT skip-loudly convention; networkidle stays BANNED on deployed targets. |

| T6130 | [A dangling working-video ref degrades into a dead Overlay screen instead of the re-export prompt](tasks/T6130-dangling-working-video-ref-degrades-badly.md) | 5 | 3 | 1.7 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-28 prod). Found independently by T6100 and T6120 during the 2026-07-27 staging E2E investigation. GET /projects/{id}/working_video/playback-url (projects.py:1172) resolves working_videos.filename from the DB and presigns it WITHOUT checking the object exists - presigning a missing key succeeds - so a dangling row returns a cheerful 200 whose URL 404s. Downstream, OverlayScreen.effectiveOverlayVideoUrl = workingVideo?.url (OverlayScreen.jsx:204) feeds the export-button gate (OverlayModeView.jsx:836); extractVideoMetadataFromUrl fails, workingVideo never hydrates, and because project.working_video_url stays truthy shouldWaitForWorkingVideo stays true, so effectiveOverlayVideoUrl stays null FOREVER - permanent spinner, export panel never mounts, retry can never succeed. The app already has the right state for this (T5440 re-export prompt); the dangling path just never reaches it. Measured blast radius (CORRECTED 2026-07-27): the first prod audit omitted the mandatory {APP_ENV}/ R2 key prefix (storage.py:279) so every HEAD 404'd and it wrongly reported prod as dangling; re-audited correctly, PROD IS CLEAN - 87 media rows, 0 dangling. Staging has 3 of 5 pre-framed drafts dangling on working_videos, AND (found via a real user report) 1 of 41 final_videos dangling - fv 41 / project 31 'Brilliant Control', which presents to the user as a black player with MEDIA_ELEMENT_ERROR: Format error; and NO code path deletes working_videos/*.mp4 (checked clips.py, overlay.py, project_archive.py, sweep) so the refs are env-copy/wipe provenance - hence Impact 5, the CAUSE is not prod-reachable but the DEGRADATION is. Task must resolve a real tension head-on: CLAUDE.md forbids defensive fixes for internal bugs, but the object store is an EXTERNAL dependency, so decide explicitly whether a dangling ref is an invariant violation to make loud or an external condition to degrade on, then build to that answer. Three candidate shapes in the task file, none pre-blessed; option 2 (fix the frontend gate so failed metadata extraction falls through to T5440) is cheapest and fixes the whole unloadable-URL class, but verify nothing else depends on shouldWaitForWorkingVideo staying true. Also decide + state whether the identical shape at clips.py:1904 needs the same treatment. Do NOT repair the prod dangling row here - that is a data op and the user's call. |
| T6140 | [removeBoundaryDuplicates self-compares the first keyframe and silently deletes it on restore](tasks/T6140-boundary-dedupe-drops-first-keyframe.md) | 8 | 2 | 4.0 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-28 prod). Found by T6050 while re-pinning keyframe-integrity.spec.js; VERIFIED independently against keyframeController.js on master before filing. removeBoundaryDuplicates (keyframeController.js:158) has ASYMMETRIC self-exclusion: the end branch guards `kf.frame < endKf.frame` so the last keyframe can never delete itself, but the start branch guards only `kf.frame > 0` - so when kf IS keyframes[0] and its frame is 1..29 (threshold = MIN_KEYFRAME_SPACING*3 = 30), hasSameSpatialData(startKf, startKf) is TRIVIALLY TRUE and the first keyframe deletes ITSELF. Triggers when: >=3 keyframes (early return covers 0-2), first keyframe at frame 1-29, and restore runs the dedupe (keyframeController.js:218). Impact 8 because this is SILENT CROP-DATA LOSS ON LOAD, not cosmetic: the function's purpose is display-only ("so two diamonds don't visually stack") but it mutates the restored list, so the user opens a clip and their first crop keyframe is gone. Reachable with ordinary data - persisted keyframes carry NO frame-0 guarantee, and under the flat-list model (permanent boundaries removed ~2026-06-21) the first keyframe is wherever the user first moved the crop box, often a low frame on a trimmed clip. Same family as T350 (origin corruption via reactive persistence) and the identity divergence healed by profile_db v014 - which is why keyframe-integrity.spec.js exists. Fix = make the start branch exclude self like the end branch already does. ALSO determine whether the de-duplicated list can be persisted back; if it can, that is a second worse bug (compounding loss per load cycle, the T350 mechanism) - report it, do not fix it here. keyframeController.test.js:793 pins the cosmetic drop as intended - give it an explicit disposition. T6050 deliberately used first-frame 35 so the spec stays honestly green while this exists; revisit INV-1/INV-3 after the fix. |
| T6150 | [Establish why staging carries dangling media refs - env-copy gap or export-durability bug?](tasks/T6150-env-copy-leaves-dangling-media-refs.md) | 7 | 3 | 2.3 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-28 prod). TWO candidate causes with very different consequences and the task must DISCRIMINATE, not assume: (1) env-copy gap - copy_user_between_envs.py copies the profile SQLite and the R2 objects from non-atomic snapshots, or filters which objects it copies; (2) export-durability bug - an export records filename and reports success while the object never durably lands, which is the T4010/T4110 sync-then-announce class, a real product defect and PROD-REACHABLE. Evidence already gathered (do not re-derive): every final-video mp4 present was written 2026-07-27 within a 39-SECOND window (18:46:15-18:46:54Z) = a bulk copy not organic exports; asymmetry of 47 mp4 objects vs 41 DB rows means 6 objects with no row AND 1 row with no object, so the two halves were not atomic; project 31 also dangles on its working_video; prod is CLEAN (87 media rows, 0 dangling, audited with the correct {APP_ENV}/users/... prefix); staging schema is at head (profile_db 29, postgres 19) and the row reads all 26 columns incl. v024/v025 additions, and a migration cannot create a missing R2 object so this is NOT a migration-window symptom. Complementary to T6130 which makes the SYMPTOM degrade gracefully; this is the SOURCE fix. Never target prod; do not repair the staging row. |
| T6160 | [A CAS conflict never self-heals - the machine can't notice R2 moved, so Retry can never succeed](tasks/T6160-cas-conflict-never-self-heals.md) | 8 | 3 | 2.7 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-28 prod). Hit LIVE by the user on staging 2026-07-27: nothing saves, framing drags snap back, saveClip 503 sync_failed, 'Could not save to the cloud - A newer version...' on every gesture. ensure_database (database.py:~657) restores from R2 on FIRST ACCESS ONLY - `if local_version is None` - with an explicit comment that a per-request HEAD is too slow (20s+ cold). So once a machine has pulled a profile DB it NEVER re-checks R2; when R2 moves ahead out-of-band (env copy, admin restore, another machine) the machine keeps its stale loaded-from version indefinitely and the T4310 upload-side CAS guard correctly refuses every write. The refusal is RIGHT and must not be weakened - the defect is the absence of RECOVERY: the offered Retry re-runs the same write with the same stale version and 503s forever, so the app tells the user to do something that cannot work, and the only escape is a restart they cannot see. Measured: R2 profile.sqlite written 22:35:31Z (db-version 2575), user tested after on a machine running since before, every gesture 503'd; after the 23:51 suspend the local copy was absent so the 00:18 restart did a clean first-access restore and writes resumed. NOT staging-only - any out-of-band R2 write reaches this state and sessions are not pinned to one machine. Fix: on CAS conflict invalidate the cached local version so the NEXT request re-pulls (one HEAD only when a conflict actually happened), decide explicitly what happens to the refused in-flight write (discard vs conflict UX - NEVER auto-merge per CLAUDE.md), fix the Retry affordance so it stops promising the impossible, and state whether user.sqlite shares the shape (memory says a live machine never re-pulls it). Do NOT add a per-request R2 HEAD. |
| T6170 | [A denormal rotation defeats the zero-check and pins the crop box - dragging snaps back](tasks/T6170-denormal-rotation-pins-crop.md) | 8 | 2 | 4.0 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-28 prod). Found by the USER reproducibly on staging 2026-07-28: framing 'Brilliant Control at Legends Mar 28' the crop square snaps back on every drag, other reels fine. MEASURED root cause (do not re-derive): working_clips.rotation for project 31 is 2.7755575615628914e-17 - a float residue, effectively zero but NOT zero - while healthy clips (projects 37, 54) store exactly 0.0. clampCropToSafeArea (utils/rotationSafeArea.js:97) early-outs only on a FALSY angle (`if (!thetaDeg) return {...crop}`), and a denormal is truthy, so the rotation-safe-area clamp runs for a ~0 rotation and pins the crop. Reproduced directly against the shipped module: crop {x:660,y:0,w:607.5,h:1080} at W=1920 H=1080 r=9/16 -> rot 0 keeps x=660, rot 2.78e-17 forces x=656.25, so every drag past 656.25 is clamped straight back. TWO defects, fix both: (1) the guard is exact-zero not epsilon, wrong test for a float coming from trig + dial arithmetic; (2) something PERSISTED a denormal instead of 0 - find it (useCrop.setRotation :327 / clampRotation, the dial nudge CropOverlay.jsx:430 setLiveRotation(prev+delta) where repeated float addition is a classic 1e-17 source, and the straighten drag->commit path; note :408 resets with a strict `rotation !== 0`). Guard-only leaves garbage in the DB; writer-only leaves every existing affected clip broken. Also check the BACKEND TWIN src/backend/app/services/rotation_safe_area.py which is documented as kept in sync - if it shares the exact-zero test the render can disagree with the editor, and a silently diverging pair is worse than either bug. State a decision on healing existing rows (>=1 exists on staging; check prod read-only first). Do NOT change the crop drag math - the drag is correct, the clamp is wrong. |

| T5960 | [Sync "Could not save to the cloud" alarm fires on a PASSIVE load (no edit made)](tasks/T5960-sync-conflict-alarm-on-passive-load.md) | 5 | 2 | 2.5 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-28 prod). Found by the 2026-07-26 staging verification sweep, observed in a real browser: on a plain load of /home/games as a real account with ZERO editing gestures, the red alarm "Could not save to the cloud / A newer version of your work exists. Retry to load it." renders (screenshot a5-01-games.png). The user is told their work could not be saved when they saved nothing. Root cause verified by code read (do NOT re-derive): the string is unique to SyncStatusIndicator.jsx:62 (syncState === 'conflict'), set from the X-Sync-Status header (syncStore.js), which reads "conflict" whenever has_sync_conflict(user_id) - a STICKY marker FILE `.sync_conflict` (database.py:88-110) that survives until a later sync succeeds, so it outlives the session whose write was refused and attaches to whatever session loads next, including a read-only one. The CAS refusal itself is working as designed - persistence-sync.md §T4310 explicitly predicts a post-deploy Retry-toast spike - so this is NOT a CAS regression; the defect is WHO gets shown the alarm. It is the adjacent case to T5870 (which stopped a 0.5s defer reading as failure DURING editing); this is a real conflict surfaced to a READER. Decision made with user 2026-07-26: gate the alarm on write-attempt - hold the conflict state but do not render it until the CURRENT session has attempted >=1 write; backend marker semantics UNCHANGED (no expiry, no CAS change). Rejected: aging out stale markers (timeout choice risks hiding a real unresolved conflict from the actual writer). Frontend-only, M-tier. Also closes an open coverage gap: the sweep could not verify "Retry clears the banner without a page refresh" because the banner never reproduced in the instrumented run. |
| T5660 | [Admin "Usage" time measured wrong (asymmetric cap) + show hours not days](tasks/T5660-admin-usage-time-measurement.md) | 4 | 3 | 1.3 | DONE | [x] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-22 prod; symmetric cap + tab-close beacon + foreground heartbeat + hours display; no backfill). Reported imankh 2026-07-20: admin Usage column looks wrong (arshia >1d, sarkarati <1d despite heavier/longer use) and "days" is confusing. Two things. **Display (trivial):** `fmtDuration` (UserTable.jsx:16) is arithmetically right (`1d`=24h) but user wants hours-only — drop the days branch, render `Nh`/`Nh Mm`. **Real bug:** `total_usage_seconds` doesn't measure engaged time. update_session (per page load `/me`) + record_milestone (per action) extend `last_active_at`; a session banks its span into total ONLY on a new-session transition (uncapped) or `/logout` via close_session (uncapped, and logout is the ONLY caller — tab-close never banks). The still-open/most-recent session is added at read time but CAPPED at 1800s (admin.py:190-199). Asymmetry = inversion: come-and-go users (arshia) bank full uncapped sessions repeatedly; heavy continuous users (sarkarati) keep one long session open→unbanked→clamped to ≤30min. Defects D1 asymmetric cap, D2 banking needs return/logout not usage, D3 read-time-only estimate never persists, D4 no idle-gap trim (≤30min idle counted active). Fix at source (symmetric shared cap constant + tab-close beacon reusing gamesDataStore pagehide seam; optional visibility-gated heartbeat = user-gated new pattern). No schema change; backfill decision open. Backend + 1-line frontend. Design-light (Architect for measurement-model call). |

| T5110 | [Poster backfill crashes on any profile below head schema](tasks/T5110-poster-backfill-crashes-below-head-schema.md) | 4 | 2 | 2.0 | DONE | [ ] | Hit on prod 2026-07-13 backfilling posters after v024. `backfill_posters` enumerates profiles via unfiltered `_get_profile_ids` (includes registry-orphans) + `ensure_database` (no migrations), so the `poster_filename IS NULL` query crashes the WHOLE run on any profile below head (2 orphan 0-reel profiles did it). run_all_migrations skips orphans by registry, so they stay behind and break the backfill - enumeration mismatch. Manual unblock: `_migrate_profile_db` each orphan first, then backfill (58/58 reels done). Fix: make backfill self-sufficient (migrate each profile to head before querying, per CLAUDE.md) and never let one bad profile abort the sweep. See memory poster-backfill-orphan-gotcha. |
| T5310 | [Registry <-> R2 profile mismatch on prod (missing + orphan profiles)](tasks/T5310-registry-r2-profile-mismatch.md) | 6 | 4 | 1.5 | DONE | [x] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-22 prod — create-profile durable-sync source fix + back-to-back regression test). Surfaced by the 2026-07-17 prod run_all_migrations (before the T4950 poster regen). Two OPPOSITE registry<->R2 mismatch directions. **Direction A "missing" (the concerning one):** the profile registry (`profiles` table in user.sqlite, via get_profiles) lists a profile but R2 has NO `profile.sqlite` object -> `_download_profile_db` found=False -> runner reason "missing". arshia.kalantari@gmail.com (937e5e54-d49a-4ebb-8b54-fdd878e15df9) has 2: profiles 22c7616a + 6ff007e6 (r2_version null). Likely create-without-sync (row written to user.sqlite before/without the first R2 upload) or a deleted/never-synced R2 object while the registry row survived -> app would load a profile with no backing data. **Direction B "orphan" (known class):** R2 dir exists, registry doesn't list it -> skipped, never migrated. imankh@gmail.com (3ed03fb5-949d-4cfd-b708-0c758ea68ef3) profile b95eb93b -- already named in the poster-backfill-orphan-gotcha memory (0-reel orphan from 2026-07-13, T5110 hand-migrated it). Migration did NOT fail on any of these (T4830's errors[]/orphans[] reporting caught them); filed to root-cause later, not fix-now. Investigation task: find why arshia's rows have no R2 object (read registry rows + R2 state directly), count full-fleet blast radius, decide fix-at-source (never register before R2 exists) vs repair vs accept, disposition the imankh orphan. No silent read-time fallback. Depth 1-2 (data integrity/sync). Related: T4830 (runner reporting), T5110 (orphan enumeration mismatch), T4820. |
| T4870 | [Admin panel shows 0 credits for users with nonzero balances](tasks/T4870-admin-credits-show-zero.md) | 5 | 3 | 1.7 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-13 prod). Reported 2026-07-10: granting N credits to a "0-credit" user returns N+8/N+6 — the balance existed, just not displayed. Root cause CONFIRMED (code read, not data loss): `get_credit_stats_for_admin` reads user.sqlite raw off local disk, silently skips missing files, and admin.py fabricates `credits_balance: 0` for skipped users (banned silent fallback); the grant path R2-restores via `ensure_user_database` first, which is why granting reveals the real balance. Fly disks are ephemeral so inactive users' files simply aren't local. Fix: route stats reads through `ensure_user_database`, null-not-zero for unreadable + `—` in UI, staleness decision (first-access restore vs per-page `sync_user_db_from_r2_if_newer`) gated on measured cost. Soft-blocks T4860 (bulk grants would act on fake zeros). |
| T4820 | [Expired-source games wrongly compute 'active' (bug 27p/29p)](tasks/T4820-expired-source-status-corruption.md) | 8 | 4 | 1.5 | DONE | [x] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-08 prod; v022+v023 migrated across all 8 prod users). Verified on prod: 6/7 corrupted games repaired incl. the 29p reporter sarkarati (games 1/3/4/6) + f05d1b29 g1 — they now compute 'expired' and show the panel. RESIDUAL (follow-up, NOT a T4820 defect): 1b842983 game 5 lives in an old profile (7ea71e41) stuck at schema v2; its 5 profiles sit at v2/3/6 and the migration runner reports them migrated while their R2 copies never advance — a pre-existing runner/sync anomaly that has starved these profiles of MANY migrations, not just v023. Hardened to expire ONLY on a confirmed R2 404, skip+warn on ambiguous errors, no-op when R2 unconfigured. bug 27p/29p. The 2026-07-04 expired-panel fix (`4b6f5bff`) is correct and deployed to prod — 29p is DATA corruption. `v017_backfill_missing_storage_refs` (bug26p heal) stamped a **fresh future** `storage_expires_at` onto every ready game missing a storage ref, **without checking the R2 source still exists**, so games whose sources the sweep had already grace-deleted now compute `_compute_storage_status`='active' forever → the panel never fires → the `<video>` 404s. Structural crack: the sweep's Phase-2 R2 delete never writes back to profile `game_storage.storage_expires_at`, so status and reality diverge. Read-only prod scan: **7 active-but-sourceless games / 3 users** (sarkarati 1/3/4/6, f05d1b29 g1 ×2 profiles, 1b842983 g5 never-tracked). Fix (user: all-users + root cause): (1) data-repair migration — for every game computing 'active' whose R2 source(s) are 404, stamp a PAST expiry (insert row if none) so it computes 'expired'; verify actual R2 state, no read-time fallback; tuple-row-factory safe. (2) root cause — sweep delete writes past expiry to profile game_storage + ref-heal path skips games whose source is confirmed gone. Migration + Architect design-gated. |
| T4830 | [Migration runner leaves some profiles stuck at old schema versions](tasks/T4830-migration-runner-skips-stale-profiles.md) | 7 | 5 | P2 | DONE | [x] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-08 prod; runner change, no schema migration needed). Dev + staging migration runs verified clean — new `errors[]`/`orphans[]` reporting live. Robust runner: registry join (migrate only registered profiles), force-download canonical R2 copy (local-ahead guard), captured sync result, verify-at-head + fail-loud; orphan cleanup script (opt-in, archive-not-delete). Prod already healed during investigation (1b842983 live profile a8428823 → v23; the 4 stale profiles are unregistered orphans, no impact). Surfaced by T4820. `run_all_migrations` had reported users migrated (`migrated:8, skipped:0`) while some profiles never advanced to head (23): prod user 1b842983 had 5 profiles stuck at user_version 2/3/6, silently missing MANY migrations (v012 inverted-clip, v014 keyframe dedup, v017-v023). `_get_profile_ids` DOES enumerate them, so the no-op is inside `_migrate_profile_db` — suspects: (1) runner migrates a stale machine-local profile.sqlite already at v23 instead of the older canonical R2 copy; (2) R2 sync version guard (`x-amz-meta-db-version`) rejects the upload so migrations apply locally but never persist; (3) these are orphan profiles the app never loads. Investigate on real data, then fix so every enumerated profile reaches head in R2 OR is explicitly excluded, and the runner FAILS LOUDLY (counts an error) when an enumerated profile didn't advance instead of silently reporting 'migrated'. Backfill stuck profiles across prod. Files: migrations/__init__.py, database.py sync_db_to_r2_explicit, storage.py db-version guard, user_db.py registry. |
| T4800 | [Reel Drafts shows 0-clip orphan drafts after a clip is deleted](tasks/T4800-zero-clip-orphan-drafts.md) | 7 | 3 | 2.3 | DONE | [x] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-08 prod). Deleting a raw clip whose auto-reel had already been exported left a 0-clip orphan draft in Reel Drafts (`_delete_auto_project` KEPT any project with a final_video_id). Root-cause fix: the dead auto-reel draft is deleted when its last source clip is deleted, preserving any PUBLISHED reel (guards on `final_videos.published_at`). Also fixes the tutorial-capture spec that produced the live orphan (project 34) by never deleting the auto-reel it created against the real account. Deliberately NO read-time clip_count==0 filter and NO client guard (they hide the bug) and NO cleanup migration (no evidence any real account is affected — a visible 0-clip draft now signals a missed producer). |
| T4175 | [Sweep Preserves Never-Framed Clips as Reel Drafts (Not Raw Published Reels)](tasks/T4175-sweep-preserve-unframed-clips-as-drafts.md) | 8 | 7 | 1.1 | DONE | [x] | DONE (deployed 2026-07-06 prod; v021 run + verified on dev + staging + prod - sarkarati fv 16-22 -> 7 frameable drafts, 0 unframed reels remain). The expiry sweep (`_export_brilliant_clip`) stream-copies each never-framed brilliant clip at original 16:9 and **publishes it as a finished reel** (then archives the auto-project) — so raw, unframed game footage sits in My Reels pretending to be a completed reel (imankh dev: reels 37-57 `auto_6_*`, "Legends March 28 8'37""; prod: sarkarati fv 16-22). T4160 stopped it overwriting framed reels and T4170/v019 fixed the aspect-ratio label, but an unframed clip should never be a finished reel — it belongs in **Reel Drafts** to be framed. Fix (one L-tier task, user decision 2026-07-05 = draft-needs-framing): (1) sweep creates/keeps a Reel Draft sourced from the preserved `auto_*.mp4` extract instead of publishing+archiving; (2) framing resolves its source to that extract when the game video is gone — **shares T4140's `resolve_clip_source`, no parallel resolver**; (3) remediation migration moves existing `auto_`-prefixed unframed published reels (dev/staging/prod) back to frameable drafts (idempotent, tuple-row-factory safe). Architecture-gated. |

| T4020 | [Export Creates Empty "Shadow" Working-Clip Version (Loses Framing)](tasks/T4020-framing-shadow-version-on-export.md) | 9 | 4 | 1.0 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-06-26 prod). After exporting a reel, the editor shows blank framing on return. Root cause (reproduced live on dev, project 39): the export->overlay transition fires a SECOND full-state save (`FramingScreen.jsx:896` `framingSaveCurrentClipState`) AFTER the export, when `useCrop`/`useSegments` have reset to defaults, persisting empty crop + default segments as a NEW working-clip version that shadows the real one (latest = `MAX(version)`). Exported video is correct; only the editor's latest version is empty. Violates "full-state saves only on explicit gesture, never reactive". Frontend-only; NOT T4010 (backend-only). Likely a core piece of the "buggy edit path". Fix test-first: guard/remove the redundant transition save. Then recover dev project 39 (drop empty working_clip v2 id 59). |
| T4010 | [Atomic Re-Export-In-Place (No Lost Final-Video References)](tasks/T4010-atomic-reexport-in-place.md) | 9 | 5 | 1.0 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-06-26 prod). Editing a published reel + re-exporting can destroy the old final video (row + R2 object + `final_video_id`) BEFORE the new one is built, with no rollback — a failed re-export leaves a "Done" reel that's unplayable + unpublishable. Reproduced on prod (project 30). Root cause: framing pre-step speculatively NULLs `final_video_id` at job-accept (framing.py:367 et al) and the failure path never restores it; `auto_export` deletes-before-inserts. Fix (no schema migration — `final_videos` is already versioned): stop the speculative NULL + restore on failure, atomic version swap, delete old R2 object only AFTER the new pointer commits, reorder auto_export. Then targeted prod recovery of project 30 (game-6 source intact, expires 2026-07-09). |

Fix: eagerly preload editor chunks on home-screen idle so the first click reuses the cached module; keep lazyWithReload+breadcrumb as fallback. Frontend-only. |

## Single-Server Priority (2026-07-18; durability re-escalated 2026-07-24) — Code Quality & Refactoring (2026-07-03 audit)

| ID | Task | Impact | Cmplx | Pri | Status | Migr | Description |
|------|------|------|------|------|------|------|------|
| T6320 | [Two progress-bar implementations - player polish misses My Reels](tasks/T6320-shared-progress-track-primitive.md) | 5 | 4 | 1.3 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-03 prod). **Found by T5130, 2026-08-01.** The sport-ball playhead shipped to DraftTile + SharedVideoOverlay but NOT to the My Reels player, and not by oversight: there are TWO independent progress-bar implementations. Verified by grep - `<MediaPlayer>` (and therefore `VideoControls`, which owns the handle) is rendered ONLY by DraftTile:726 and SharedVideoOverlay:99, while DownloadsPanel:522 (My Reels) renders `CollectionPlayer`, whose segmented bar (`reels.map` of `<button>` segments, h-1 white fill) **has no playhead handle at all**. T5130's own task file listed DownloadsPanel/RankingGame/ProjectManager as consumers to thread the sport prop through - that list was STALE, none of them render MediaPlayer, so the criterion "works on the author's My Reels player" was unmeetable without this. Cost is recurring: every future player improvement must be written twice or silently applies to only some surfaces. Extract a store-free `ProgressTrack` + `PlayheadHandle` into `components/shared/`; VideoControls composes them full-width, CollectionPlayer composes per segment and renders the handle on the ACTIVE segment (that is what delivers the ball to My Reels). **Design gate:** the two bars look different today (purple 3-6px continuous vs white 1px segmented) - parameterize (recommended, no visual change) vs converge visuals (restyles My Reels, nobody asked for it); get the choice approved, do not silently restyle. **CRITICAL:** the primitive is imported by VideoControls, which the landing site pulls via the `@editor` alias, so it MUST stay store-free and live under `components/shared/`; verify the `src/landing` build. Honest caveat: this is the 2nd duplication, not the 3rd (CLAUDE.md says abstract on the 3rd) - going early is justified only because the duplication already swallowed a shipped feature; keep the primitive small, and if it grows options to serve both callers that is the signal to leave them separate. **Sequencing: must branch from the T5130 commit** (the handle code being extracted), i.e. after `feature/T6310-frontend-tile-polish` lands on master. Characterization tests first, strangler-fig, mechanical moves only. |

| T3760 | [Framing Clip Cold-Load Over-Fetch](tasks/T3760-framing-clip-cold-load-overfetch.md) | 9 | 5 | 1.8 | DONE | [ ] | **Closed WON'T-FIX (spike, 2026-06-18):** the over-fetch is real but harmless. Measured against the real prod object (clip 48, 3.05 GB, confirmed faststart): cold time-to-first-frame after a deep seek = **266 ms** (warm 16 ms); browser buffers only ~3s (~1.85 MB transferred cold); seeks ~300 ms **even under 8-socket saturation**; R2 TTFB 82-151 ms. The "4.3s / 55.8s / 1.03 GB" were a HAR misread (`receive` time != TTFF; advertised `Content-Length` != bytes downloaded). No `Content-Length` clamp can improve a 266 ms TTFF. Decision doc: [T3760-decision.md](tasks/T3760-decision.md). Resolves T2560 kept-skip; re-scopes T2550/epic off latency. |

Carved into T3775; no code changed here. |

Shipped commit 9f06b784, deployed 2026-06-18. |

## Current Focus — Milestone: Deploy Candidate

| ID | Task | Impact | Cmplx | Pri | Status | Migr | Description |

| T6920 | [Attached intro card missing from every DOWNLOADED reel on Fly - import-time IndexError in intro_card_geometry](tasks/T6920-intro-card-missing-from-downloads-fly-layout.md) | 8 | 3 | 2.7 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-13 prod). **Filed 2026-08-12 from user report (staging, imankh@gmail.com, reel 38 "Brilliant Dribble and Assist"), LIVE-REPRODUCED + root-caused at filing.** Preview/in-app play shows the intro card; the downloaded file starts straight on footage (frames extracted) and carries only the outro. Staging traceback captured: `intro_card_geometry.py:596` computes the JS-mirror path `Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]` at MODULE IMPORT - in the Fly image (`/app/app/services/...`) only 4 parents exist, so importing `player_intro` raises `IndexError: 4` on EVERY burn, swallowed by the non-fatal ladder. Broken on Fly since T5210 shipped (2026-08-05); masked until T6860 fixed the R2-image rung above it (verified: the image now downloads, then the import dies). Affects owner download + share download; playback egresses never import the Python renderer, hence the asymmetry. Fix: lazy `_JS_PATH` (dev-only tooling) + shallow-path import regression test. **Verification (explicit user requirement): drive Playwright as the real account over ALL reels x {hover-preview, full play, download, copy-link playback, shared-page download} with frame-level evidence - matrix + per-surface expected behavior in the task file.** |
| T6860 | [Attached intro card neither plays back in-app nor appears in the download](tasks/T6860-attached-intro-card-not-playing-or-downloading.md) | 8 | 4 | 2.0 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-13 prod). **Merged 2026-08-12, 4 rounds of real live-repro on staging.** (1) Download egress fixed: burn path's R2 image download used a transfer client that failed non-transiently on Fly (not reproducible off-Fly under 2 ffmpeg versions) - switched to the sync client. (2) Same-reel-different-card-per-egress fixed: a Fly multi-machine staleness bug - the burn/download path read the athlete's full_name from a possibly-stale local user.sqlite (restore-if-ABSENT only) while playback read from a fresher machine - fixed by freshening user.sqlite (restore-if-newer) before reading facts. (3) "Multiple cards during playback" and (4) "preview shouldn't include intro" were BOTH live-reproduced as non-defects: a single reel's playback never shows more than one card, and every hover/quick-preview surface already excludes the intro (only full in-app playback + downloads compose it, by design, T6700/T6710) - guard test added to pin this. 151+ tests green across all rounds. |
| T6880 | [Overlay text renders with playhead outside the region's range](tasks/T6880-selected-text-region-renders-outside-range.md) | 6 | 3 | 2.0 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-13 prod). **Top-area bug (user-reported 2026-08-11, screenshot in hand).** Playhead past a text region's end still renders its text on the preview canvas, while the Text panel simultaneously says "No text region under the playhead". Mechanism ALREADY LOCATED at filing: `TextOverlayPreview.jsx` (~159-163) renders the SELECTED region even out of range - a deliberate editing affordance (header comment) that (a) contradicts the panel's strict containment, (b) shows out-of-range text during playback, (c) breaks the file's own "preview == export by construction" claim (text_render.py burns strict windows). Fix = unwind the design decision: preferred option renders the out-of-range selected region only as a visually-distinct editing ghost while paused on the Text tab (never during playback), with ONE shared containment predicate for panel + canvas; alternative snaps the playhead into a region on select and drops the exception. Interacts with T6720's grab-frame drag. Real-browser verify against the screenshot's exact setup. |
| T6830 | [New-user home: default Games tab + disable dead-end Reel Drafts](tasks/T6830-new-user-home-tab-defaults.md) | 5 | 2 | 2.5 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-13 prod). Filed 2026-08-11. A user who has never uploaded a game must land on the Games tab, and when there are no draft reels AND "New Reel" is disabled (no game has extracted clips), the Reel Drafts tab button is disabled too (tooltip explains why) - clicking it can only show an empty list with a dead action button. Derived per render from the existing `hasClips` gate + `projects.length`; no new state, no persistence. Includes `/home/reels` deep-link fallback in the disabled state without regressing T5677's URL-authoritative tabs. Frontend-only, `ProjectManager.jsx`. |

| T6850 | [Remove "Minimum reel length" setting + data](tasks/T6850-remove-min-reel-length-setting.md) | 4 | 3 | 1.3 | DONE | [x] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-13 prod). Remove end to end: ProfileIntroSection field + introCardStore state, both profiles.py endpoints, intro_cards.py helpers + stale duration-gate comments, fresh-DDL column, NEW profile_db v043 drop-column migration (collision-check the number against unmerged branches first; idempotent, absent-column safe). Code stops reading before the drop lands (reads already `column_exists()`-guarded). Needs Migration agent + POST /api/admin/migrate after deploy. |
| T6870 | [Overlay always opens scrolled - should start at scroll 0](tasks/T6870-overlay-opens-scrolled.md) | 5 | 2 | 2.5 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-13 prod). **Merged 2 rounds, 2026-08-12.** Round 1 (3e3a88b8) fixed page-level vertical scroll (scrollIntoView on PosterMarkerLayer's mount-reveal replaced with horizontal-only scrollTo on the timeline's own container). Round-2 user evidence (screenshot) showed the timeline's OWN horizontal scrollbar still not flush-left on launch - root-caused to the T6630 poster-marker reveal centering unconditionally even when the marker was already visible. User chose "reveal only when off-screen, minimal scroll" (2bdf3950): reuses `computeFollowScrollTarget`, verified live at 171%/403% zoom that an already-visible marker now leaves scroll at 0 while a genuinely off-screen marker still gets revealed. |
| T6900 | [Draft reel card should stay in source aspect until framing is actually applied](tasks/T6900-draft-tile-source-aspect-until-framed.md) | 5 | 3 | 1.7 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-13 prod). Filed 2026-08-12 from user report. T6800/T6810 (merged, STAGING) already force source aspect for `NOT_STARTED` drafts, but `getDraftStage` buckets a draft into `IN_FRAMING` the moment clips are extracted - BEFORE any crop/framing keyframes exist - and `DraftTile.jsx`'s aspect decision only checks `isNotStarted`, so an entered-but-unframed draft falls through to the target (portrait) aspect. Extend the landscape override to cover IN_FRAMING-but-no-crop-yet, switching to target aspect once real framing exists. **Verified 2026-08-12 in a fresh Docker stack + Playwright against imankh@gmail.com's real (restored) production data: "Good Buildup" (In Framing, no crop) rendered landscape; "Good Control" and other cropped/Ready reels rendered target aspect. No migration needed - `has_crop_keyframes` derives cleanly from existing `crop_data`.** |
| T6930 | [Intro card store survives profile switch - wrong card gets attached and plays](tasks/intro-rebuild/T6930-card-store-profile-switch-reset.md) | 7 | 2 | 3.5 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-13 prod). **Filed 2026-08-12 from user report ("attached a card, a different one played"), root-caused by a 3-agent audit — most likely mechanism.** `useIntroCardStore` is missing from `profileStore._resetDataStores()` and its `reset()` has zero call sites; card ids are per-profile AUTOINCREMENT, so after a profile switch the picker can render the OLD profile's cards and the attach PATCH validates that id against the NEW profile's table, where it exists as a different card — no error anywhere. Fix: enroll the store in the teardown + stamp `cardsProfileId` on fetch so the in-flight window renders loading, never a stale list. Frontend-only. Investigation record: [intro-rebuild/EPIC.md](tasks/intro-rebuild/EPIC.md) — user decided bug-fixes-only, no redesign (attachment stays at the last step; serve-time model stands). |
| T6940 | [Intro card picker exits: backdrop close + create-without-attach dead ends](tasks/intro-rebuild/T6940-picker-exit-dead-ends.md) | 5 | 2 | 2.5 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-13 prod). Filed 2026-08-12 (audit mechanism M2). The picker closes on backdrop click (violates the standing no-backdrop-close rule) and the inline create flow can exit via header X / backdrop with the new card created but NEVER attached — the user believes they added an intro; the old card keeps playing. Fix: remove backdrop-close, ensure the freshly created card is the selected draft, confirm-on-exit when a just-created card would be left unattached. Frontend-only. |
| T6950 | [Intro card: one image rule + card-delete must clear stale badges](tasks/intro-rebuild/T6950-one-image-rule-one-name-cache.md) | 4 | 2 | 2.0 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-13 prod). Filed 2026-08-12 (audit M5+M6). (a) Egress resolves the card image cutout-first while editor/preview presign `image_key` only — retire the never-written `image_cutout_key` semantics so ONE rule exists. (b) Card DELETE nulls `final_videos.intro_card_id` server-side but none of the three frontend caches carrying `intro_card_name` — badges keep naming a deleted card until reload; invalidate via refetch on delete. |
| T6960 | [Intro card photo must be ready before the intro clock starts](tasks/intro-rebuild/T6960-intro-image-ready-before-play.md) | 6 | 3 | 2.0 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-13 prod). **Filed 2026-08-13 from staging testing (imankh@gmail.com): card animates without its photo, flaky across replays.** Two-layer cause: the intro clock (`useIntroPlayback`) starts at mount and never waits for the photo, AND every play presigns a FRESH R2 URL (new signature query params) so the browser cache never helps — each play re-races a 1-2MB download against a 4s card. Fix: `preloadIntroImage` util (decode + 2.5s cap, always resolves, warns on error/timeout) gating the composite's play switch, the share-SPA pre-roll's autoplay, and the edge page's animation start. Cache-stable image URLs noted as future work, not in scope. |
| T6970 | [Edge share page intro card fills the viewport instead of the video canvas](tasks/intro-rebuild/T6970-edge-share-intro-sizing.md) | 5 | 2 | 2.5 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-13 prod). Filed 2026-08-13 from staging screenshot (incognito share link): `#intro-card{inset:0}` covers all of `<main>` while the video is a centered aspect-fitted box. Fix: JS `sizeIntro()` matches the card to `video.getBoundingClientRect()` (initial + loadedmetadata + resize) and scales the name/fact font sizes to the box. Same file as T6960's edge-page gate — same branch, separate commits. |
| T6910 | [user.sqlite R2 restore silently skipped when local file is gone but version cache is stale](tasks/T6910-user-db-restore-skip-stale-version-cache.md) | 7 | 2 | 3.5 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-13 prod). Root-caused 2026-08-12 while unblocking T6900's manual verification against imankh@gmail.com's real copied-down prod account, which looked blank after a prod->dev copy + manual `rm -rf` of the local cache dir. `ensure_user_database` (`user_db.py:122-217`) gates its R2 restore on ONLY the in-process, memory-only `_user_sqlite_versions` cache being unset - if a process already has a non-None cached version and the local file is deleted out-of-band (not via `forget_user_db`), the restore is skipped entirely and a brand-new BLANK `user.sqlite` silently replaces the real one. Fix: also gate on `not db_path.exists()`. **Actual mechanism: two orphaned `uvicorn --reload` worker processes (spawned via `multiprocessing`, one predating this session) kept serving :8000 with stale in-memory caches through every "restart" attempt - identified and killed during verification. Fix verified live: re-copied prod->dev, confirmed a genuinely fresh process correctly restores profile `9fa7378c` (47 real projects) instead of fabricating a blank one.** |

No shared rename component exists today - implementer decides whether to extract one or fix positioning per-surface. Frontend-only, ~4-5 files. |
| T6980 | [Overlay: double-click/double-tap a text element edits it in place (+ Text tab sync)](tasks/T6980-overlay-double-click-text-inline-edit.md) | 6 | 5 | 1.2 | DONE | [ ] | DONE (deployed 2026-08-16 prod).
